TL;DR: 提出LLM驱动的多智能体仿真框架，用于优化服务运营，通过将设计选择嵌入提示词来建模决策依赖的不确定性，开发了单次仿真中同时构建梯度估计和更新参数的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务系统性能依赖于参与者对设计选择的响应，但由于人类行为的复杂性，建模这些响应非常困难。需要一种能够有效模拟人类行为响应并优化服务运营的方法。

Method: 提出LLM-MAS框架：1) 将问题建模为具有决策依赖不确定性的随机优化问题；2) 将设计选择嵌入提示词，通过LLM智能体交互形成结果分布；3) 在提示词中嵌入关键数值信息并从LLM生成文本中提取；4) 将不确定性建模为受控马尔可夫链；5) 开发on-trajectory学习算法，在单次仿真运行中同时构建零阶梯度估计并更新设计参数；6) 结合方差减少技术。

Result: 在可持续供应链应用中，该方法优于基准方法（包括黑盒优化、使用LLM作为数值求解器或角色扮演系统设计者）。在基于真实行为数据的最优竞赛设计案例研究中，LLM-MAS既能作为已知设计的成本效益评估器，又能作为探索性工具，发现传统方法忽略的优秀设计。

Conclusion: LLM-MAS框架为优化服务运营提供了一种有效方法，能够建模复杂的人类行为响应，并在实际应用中展现出优于传统方法的性能，既能评估现有设计，又能探索新的优化方案。

Abstract: Service system performance depends on how participants respond to design choices, but modeling these responses is hard due to the complexity of human behavior. We introduce an LLM-powered multi-agent simulation (LLM-MAS) framework for optimizing service operations. We pose the problem as stochastic optimization with decision-dependent uncertainty: design choices are embedded in prompts and shape the distribution of outcomes from interacting LLM-powered agents. By embedding key numerical information in prompts and extracting it from LLM-generated text, we model this uncertainty as a controlled Markov chain. We develop an on-trajectory learning algorithm that, on a single simulation run, simultaneously constructs zeroth-order gradient estimates and updates design parameters to optimize steady-state performance. We also incorporate variance reduction techniques. In a sustainable supply chain application, our method outperforms benchmarks, including blackbox optimization and using LLMs as numerical solvers or as role-playing system designers. A case study on optimal contest design with real behavioral data shows that LLM-MAS is both as a cost-effective evaluator of known designs and an exploratory tool that can uncover strong designs overlooked by traditional approaches.

</details>


### [187] [Automatically Generating Hard Math Problems from Hypothesis-Driven Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04386)
*Jiayu Fu,Mourad Heddaya,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于AI生成假设的数学基准测试生成管道，能识别LLM易错的具体数学概念和技能，并针对性生成测试问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准测试大多依赖人工构建，难以扩展，无法跟上LLM发展速度，且不能有效识别LLM在具体数学概念和技能上的弱点

Method: 使用AI生成假设来识别LLM易错的数学概念和技能，然后针对这些弱点生成新的基准测试问题

Result: 假设准确性与生成问题难度正相关：基于最准确假设生成的问题将Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct的准确率降至45%（原MATH基准为77%），且管道具有高度适应性

Conclusion: 该管道不仅能有效评估LLM数学能力，还可扩展到其他领域探索LLM能力，成为研究LLM跨领域表现的有价值工具

Abstract: Numerous math benchmarks exist to evaluate LLMs' mathematical capabilities. However, most involve extensive manual effort and are difficult to scale. Consequently, they cannot keep pace with LLM development or easily provide new instances to mitigate overfitting. Some researchers have proposed automatic benchmark generation methods, but few focus on identifying the specific math concepts and skills on which LLMs are error-prone, and most can only generate category-specific benchmarks. To address these limitations, we propose a new math benchmark generation pipeline that uses AI-generated hypotheses to identify the specific math concepts and skills that LLMs struggle with, and then generates new benchmark problems targeting these weaknesses. Experiments show that hypothesis accuracy positively correlates with the difficulty of the generated problems: problems generated from the most accurate hypotheses reduce Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct's accuracy to as low as 45%, compared to 77% on the original MATH benchmark. Furthermore, our pipeline is highly adaptable and can be applied beyond math to explore a wide range of LLM capabilities, making it a valuable tool for investigating how LLMs perform across different domains.

</details>


### [188] [MolDA: Molecular Understanding and Generation via Large Language Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04403)
*Seohyeon Shin,HanJun Choi,Jun-Hyung Park,Hongkook Kim,Mansu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MolDA：基于掩码扩散的分子多模态框架，用离散大语言扩散模型替代传统自回归主干，通过双向迭代去噪确保分子全局结构一致性和化学有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归主干的多模态分子架构存在局限性：严格从左到右的归纳偏差不适合生成化学有效分子，难以处理非局部全局约束（如环闭合），且在序列生成过程中容易累积结构错误

Method: 提出MolDA框架：1）用离散大语言扩散模型替代传统自回归主干；2）使用混合图编码器提取全面的结构表示，捕获局部和全局拓扑；3）通过Q-Former将结构表示对齐到语言标记空间；4）为掩码扩散专门数学重构分子结构偏好优化；5）通过双向迭代去噪确保全局结构一致性

Result: MolDA在分子生成、描述和性质预测任务中展现出全局结构一致性、化学有效性和鲁棒推理能力

Conclusion: MolDA通过掩码扩散框架克服了自回归模型在分子生成中的局限性，为多模态分子发现提供了更有效的解决方案，特别在处理全局结构约束和化学有效性方面表现出色

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced molecular discovery, but existing multimodal molecular architectures fundamentally rely on autoregressive (AR) backbones. This strict left-to-right inductive bias is sub-optimal for generating chemically valid molecules, as it struggles to account for non-local global constraints (e.g., ring closures) and often accumulates structural errors during sequential generation. To address these limitations, we propose MolDA (Molecular language model with masked Diffusion with mAsking), a novel multimodal framework that replaces the conventional AR backbone with a discrete Large Language Diffusion Model. MolDA extracts comprehensive structural representations using a hybrid graph encoder, which captures both local and global topologies, and aligns them into the language token space via a Q-Former. Furthermore, we mathematically reformulate Molecular Structure Preference Optimization specifically for the masked diffusion. Through bidirectional iterative denoising, MolDA ensures global structural coherence, chemical validity, and robust reasoning across molecule generation, captioning, and property prediction.

</details>


### [189] [Empirical Characterization of Rationale Stability Under Controlled Perturbations for Explainable Pattern Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04456)
*Abu Noman Md Sakib,Zhensen Wang,Merjulah Roby,Zijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种评估模型解释一致性的新指标，通过量化相同标签输入之间SHAP值的余弦相似度，检测模型在相似输入上的解释稳定性，用于构建更可靠的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI评估大多局限于单个实例，缺乏对跨样本解释一致性的量化评估。可靠的模式识别系统应在相似输入上表现一致，其解释也应保持稳定，但当前方法无法有效检测模型是否对同类输入使用一致的推理模式。

Method: 提出基于余弦相似度的解释一致性度量指标，使用预训练的BERT模型在SST-2情感分析数据集上实现，并在RoBERTa、DistilBERT和IMDB数据集上进行鲁棒性测试。应用SHAP计算特征重要性，量化相同标签输入之间SHAP值的相似度，检测不一致行为如对特定特征的偏见依赖或相似预测的推理不一致。

Result: 通过一系列实验评估了该指标识别错位预测和模型解释不一致的能力，并与标准保真度指标进行比较。实验表明该指标能有效检测模型行为偏离预期目标的情况，为模型行为提供更深入的理解。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过实现更稳健的推理稳定性验证，为构建可信AI系统提供了关键工具。通过量化模型在相似输入上是否依赖一致的归因模式，该方法支持在实际模式识别管道中对模型行为进行更稳健的评估。

Abstract: Reliable pattern recognition systems should exhibit consistent behavior across similar inputs, and their explanations should remain stable. However, most Explainable AI evaluations remain instance centric and do not explicitly quantify whether attribution patterns are consistent across samples that share the same class or represent small variations of the same input. In this work, we propose a novel metric aimed at assessing the consistency of model explanations, ensuring that models consistently reflect the intended objectives and consistency under label-preserving perturbations. We implement this metric using a pre-trained BERT model on the SST-2 sentiment analysis dataset, with additional robustness tests on RoBERTa, DistilBERT, and IMDB, applying SHAP to compute feature importance for various test samples. The proposed metric quantifies the cosine similarity of SHAP values for inputs with the same label, aiming to detect inconsistent behaviors, such as biased reliance on certain features or failure to maintain consistent reasoning for similar predictions. Through a series of experiments, we evaluate the ability of this metric to identify misaligned predictions and inconsistencies in model explanations. These experiments are compared against standard fidelity metrics to assess whether the new metric can effectively identify when a model's behavior deviates from its intended objectives. The proposed framework provides a deeper understanding of model behavior by enabling more robust verification of rationale stability, which is critical for building trustworthy AI systems. By quantifying whether models rely on consistent attribution patterns for similar inputs, the proposed approach supports more robust evaluation of model behavior in practical pattern recognition pipelines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/anmspro/ESS-XAI-Stability.

</details>


### [190] [The Topology of Multimodal Fusion: Why Current Architectures Fail at Creative Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04465)
*Xiujiang Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文识别出现有多模态AI架构中的拓扑结构限制，提出基于中国工艺认识论的"象"概念和十字框架，通过哲学、认知科学和数学三支柱重新构建多模态融合理论，并提出UOO实现方法和新评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态AI架构（如CLIP、GPT-4V/Gemini、扩散模型）存在模态可分离性的拓扑结构限制，这源于西方哲学传统中的"说/显"二分法。论文旨在通过中国工艺认识论的"象"概念突破这一限制，实现真正的多模态融合。

Method: 1. 哲学支柱：重新诠释维特根斯坦的"说/显"区分，引入中国工艺认识论的"象"概念，建立十字框架（道/器 × 说/显）；2. 认知科学支柱：通过病理镜像重新解释DMN/ECN/SN三系统共激活；3. 数学支柱：使用纤维丛和杨-米尔斯曲率形式化；4. 提出UOO实现（带拓扑正则化的神经ODE）、ANALOGY-MM基准和META-TOP三层次基准。

Result: 提出了一个全新的多模态融合理论框架，包括：1. 十字结构理论；2. 双层次动力学（创化与化裁）；3. 病理镜像的2D参数空间分析；4. 数学形式化；5. 具体实现方案和评测基准；6. 分阶段实验路线图。

Conclusion: 论文通过跨文明视角（中国工艺认识论与西方哲学对话）重新构建了多模态AI的理论基础，提出了超越当前模态可分离性限制的新架构范式，并为验证这一理论设计了完整的实验和评测体系。

Abstract: This paper identifies a structural limitation in current multimodal AI architectures that is topological rather than parametric. Contrastive alignment (CLIP), cross-attention fusion (GPT-4V/Gemini), and diffusion-based generation share a common geometric prior -- modal separability -- which we term contact topology. The argument rests on three pillars with philosophy as the generative center. The philosophical pillar reinterprets Wittgenstein's saying/showing distinction as a problem rather than a conclusion: where Wittgenstein chose silence, the Chinese craft epistemology tradition responded with xiang (operative schema) -- the third state emerging when saying and showing interpenetrate. A cruciform framework (dao/qi x saying/showing) positions xiang at the intersection, executing dual huacai (transformation-and-cutting) along both axes. This generates a dual-layer dynamics: chuanghua (creative transformation as spontaneous event) and huacai (its institutionalization into repeatable form). The cognitive science pillar reinterprets DMN/ECN/SN tripartite co-activation through the pathological mirror: overlap isomorphism vs. superimposition collapse in a 2D parameter space (coupling intensity x regulatory capacity). The mathematical pillar formalizes these via fiber bundles and Yang-Mills curvature, with the cruciform structure mapped to fiber bundle language. We propose UOO implementation via Neural ODEs with topological regularization, the ANALOGY-MM benchmark with error-type-ratio metric, and the META-TOP three-tier benchmark testing cross-civilizational topological isomorphism across seven archetypes. A phased experimental roadmap with explicit termination criteria ensures clean exit if falsified.

</details>


### [191] [What Makes a Sale? Rethinking End-to-End Seller--Buyer Retail Dynamics with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04468)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Jibin Hwang,Gyeonghun Sun,Minjeong Ban,Taewon Yun,Hyeonjae Cheon,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RetailSim是一个端到端的零售模拟框架，统一建模从卖家说服到买家决策的多阶段零售流程，通过双重评估协议验证其行为保真度和经济规律再现能力，并展示其在零售策略评估中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售模拟器仅捕捉零售流程的部分方面，缺乏对跨阶段依赖关系的建模，难以评估早期决策如何影响下游结果，因此需要开发能够统一建模整个零售管道的端到端模拟框架。

Method: 提出RetailSim框架，通过多样化的产品空间、角色驱动的智能体和多轮交互实现模拟保真度；采用双重评估协议：人类评估行为保真度和基于真实世界经济规律的元评估。

Result: RetailSim成功再现了关键经济模式，包括人口统计购买行为、价格-需求关系以及异质性价格弹性；在角色推断、卖家-买家交互分析和销售策略评估等决策导向用例中展示了实用价值。

Conclusion: RetailSim作为一个受控测试平台，能够有效探索零售策略，通过端到端的统一建模和验证的模拟保真度，为零售策略部署前的评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Evaluating retail strategies before deployment is difficult, as outcomes are determined across multiple stages, from seller-side persuasion through buyer-seller interaction to purchase decisions. However, existing retail simulators capture only partial aspects of this process and do not model cross-stage dependencies, making it difficult to assess how early decisions affect downstream outcomes. We present RetailSim, an end-to-end retail simulation framework that models this pipeline in a unified environment, explicitly designed for simulation fidelity through diverse product spaces, persona-driven agents, and multi-turn interactions. We evaluate RetailSim with a dual protocol comprising human evaluation of behavioral fidelity and meta-evaluation against real-world economic regularities, showing that it successfully reproduces key patterns such as demographic purchasing behavior, the price-demand relationship, and heterogeneous price elasticity. We further demonstrate its practical utility via decision-oriented use cases, including persona inference, seller-buyer interaction analysis, and sales strategy evaluation, showing RetailSim's potential as a controlled testbed for exploring retail strategies.

</details>


### [192] [Same World, Differently Given: History-Dependent Perceptual Reorganization in Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04637)
*Hongju Pae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种最小化架构，通过慢速视角潜变量g的反馈机制，使人工智能体能够维持历史敏感的世界观，实现相同观察因累积立场而不同编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索何种内部组织能让智能体不仅适应行为，还能维持对世界的历史敏感视角，解决传统智能体缺乏持续历史视角的问题。

Method: 设计最小化架构：慢速视角潜变量g反馈到感知系统，同时通过感知处理更新自身；在固定空间支架和感觉扰动的网格世界中进行评估。

Result: 1) 扰动历史在名义条件恢复后留下可测量的适应可塑性残留；2) 视角潜变量重组感知编码，相同观察因先前经验而不同表示；3) 仅自适应自调节产生特征性的增长-稳定动态。

Conclusion: 该架构为人工智能体中的历史依赖视角组织提供了最小机制，主要重组发生在感知层面而非行为层面，行为保持稳定。

Abstract: What kind of internal organization would allow an artificial agent not only to adapt its behavior, but to sustain a history-sensitive perspective on its world? I present a minimal architecture in which a slow perspective latent $g$ feeds back into perception and is itself updated through perceptual processing. This allows identical observations to be encoded differently depending on the agent's accumulated stance. The model is evaluated in a minimal gridworld with a fixed spatial scaffold and sensory perturbations. Across analyses, three results emerge: first, perturbation history leaves measurable residue in adaptive plasticity after nominal conditions are restored. Second, the perspective latent reorganizes perceptual encoding, such that identical observations are represented differently depending on prior experience. Third, only adaptive self-modulation yields the characteristic growth-then-stabilization dynamic, unlike rigid or always-open update regimes. Gross behavior remains stable throughout, suggesting that the dominant reorganization is perceptual rather than behavioral. Together, these findings identify a minimal mechanism for history-dependent perspectival organization in artificial agents.

</details>


### [193] [On the "Causality" Step in Policy Gradient Derivations: A Pedagogical Reconciliation of Full Return and Reward-to-Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04686)
*Nima H. Siboni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对策略梯度中REINFORCE估计器的推导进行了数学严谨性改进，通过前缀轨迹分布和得分函数恒等式，明确展示了"奖励到终点"如何自然产生，而非作为事后无偏替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略梯度的入门介绍中，通常使用完整轨迹回报推导REINFORCE估计器，然后基于"因果性"声称完整回报可被"奖励到终点"替代。虽然这一说法正确，但严谨性不足，未能清晰说明过去奖励项如何消失。本文旨在提供更数学明确的推导。

Method: 采用前缀轨迹分布和得分函数恒等式进行推导。通过将目标函数分解为前缀轨迹的形式，直接从数学上展示"奖励到终点"如何自然产生，而非作为事后添加的无偏替代。

Result: 推导出的估计器与传统REINFORCE估计器相同，但提供了更严谨的数学基础。将通常的因果性论证恢复为推导的推论，而非额外的启发式原则。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是概念性的：通过前缀轨迹分解，展示了"奖励到终点"如何直接从目标函数中产生，为策略梯度推导提供了更严谨的数学框架，使因果性论证成为推导的自然结果而非外部假设。

Abstract: In introductory presentations of policy gradients, one often derives the REINFORCE estimator using the full trajectory return and then states, by ``causality,'' that the full return may be replaced by the reward-to-go. Although this statement is correct, it is frequently presented at a level of rigor that leaves unclear where the past-reward terms disappear. This short paper isolates that step and gives a mathematically explicit derivation based on prefix trajectory distributions and the score-function identity. The resulting account does not change the estimator. Its contribution is conceptual: instead of presenting reward-to-go as a post hoc unbiased replacement for full return, it shows that reward-to-go arises directly once the objective is decomposed over prefix trajectories. In this formulation, the usual causality argument is recovered as a corollary of the derivation rather than as an additional heuristic principle.

</details>


### [194] [AI Assistance Reduces Persistence and Hurts Independent Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04721)
*Grace Liu,Brian Christian,Tsvetomira Dumbalska,Michiel A. Bakker,Rachit Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI助手虽然短期提升任务表现，但会降低用户坚持性和无协助时的表现能力，这对长期技能习得构成风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统被设计为提供即时完整回答的短期合作者，而人类导师则通过搭建学习框架、跟踪进度、优先考虑对方成长来实现长期目标合作。研究旨在探究AI协助对用户坚持性和无协助表现的影响后果。

Method: 通过一系列随机对照试验（N=1,222）研究人机交互，涵盖数学推理和阅读理解等多种任务，观察AI协助对用户表现的影响。

Result: AI协助短期内确实提升表现，但导致用户在无协助时表现显著变差且更容易放弃。这些效应在仅约10分钟的AI交互后即出现。坚持性降低是因为AI让用户习惯于即时答案，剥夺了独立应对挑战的经验。

Conclusion: AI模型开发需要优先考虑搭建长期能力框架，而不仅仅是即时任务完成，因为坚持性是技能习得的基础和长期学习的最强预测因素之一。

Abstract: People often optimize for long-term goals in collaboration: A mentor or companion doesn't just answer questions, but also scaffolds learning, tracks progress, and prioritizes the other person's growth over immediate results. In contrast, current AI systems are fundamentally short-sighted collaborators - optimized for providing instant and complete responses, without ever saying no (unless for safety reasons). What are the consequences of this dynamic? Here, through a series of randomized controlled trials on human-AI interactions (N = 1,222), we provide causal evidence for two key consequences of AI assistance: reduced persistence and impairment of unassisted performance. Across a variety of tasks, including mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension, we find that although AI assistance improves performance in the short-term, people perform significantly worse without AI and are more likely to give up. Notably, these effects emerge after only brief interactions with AI (approximately 10 minutes). These findings are particularly concerning because persistence is foundational to skill acquisition and is one of the strongest predictors of long-term learning. We posit that persistence is reduced because AI conditions people to expect immediate answers, thereby denying them the experience of working through challenges on their own. These results suggest the need for AI model development to prioritize scaffolding long-term competence alongside immediate task completion.

</details>


### [195] [Incompleteness of AI Safety Verification via Kolmogorov Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04876)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明AI安全验证存在根本性信息理论限制：任何有限形式验证器都无法认证所有高复杂度的策略合规实例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在安全关键领域需要满足形式化安全和策略约束，但现有验证方法存在局限性。传统认为这些限制源于组合复杂性和模型表达能力，但本文旨在揭示其内在的信息理论根源。

Method: 将策略合规性形式化为编码系统行为的验证问题，使用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度进行分析。证明了一个不完备性结果：对于任何固定的、可计算的、可枚举的验证器，存在一个阈值，超过该阈值后，真实的策略合规实例一旦其复杂度超过该阈值就无法被认证。

Result: 证明了有限形式验证器无法认证所有任意高复杂度的策略合规实例。这一限制独立于计算资源，揭示了AI安全验证的根本性信息理论限制。

Conclusion: AI安全验证存在固有的信息理论限制，这为证明携带方法提供了动机，后者可以提供实例级别的正确性保证。

Abstract: Ensuring that artificial intelligence (AI) systems satisfy formal safety and policy constraints is a central challenge in safety-critical domains. While limitations of verification are often attributed to combinatorial complexity and model expressiveness, we show that they arise from intrinsic information-theoretic limits. We formalize policy compliance as a verification problem over encoded system behaviors and analyze it using Kolmogorov complexity. We prove an incompleteness result: for any fixed sound computably enumerable verifier, there exists a threshold beyond which true policy-compliant instances cannot be certified once their complexity exceeds that threshold. Consequently, no finite formal verifier can certify all policy-compliant instances of arbitrarily high complexity. This reveals a fundamental limitation of AI safety verification independent of computational resources, and motivates proof-carrying approaches that provide instance-level correctness guarantees.

</details>


### [196] [Learning, Potential, and Retention: An Approach for Evaluating Adaptive AI-Enabled Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04878)
*Alexis Burgon,Berkman Sahiner,Nicholas A Petrick,Gene Pennello,Ravi K Samala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估医疗设备自适应AI模型的新方法，包含学习、潜力和保留三个互补指标，用于区分模型适应与动态环境变化对性能的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备中自适应AI模型的迭代更新（模型和评估数据集同时变化）使得性能评估变得复杂，需要区分性能变化是由模型适应还是动态环境变化引起的

Method: 引入三个互补测量指标：学习（模型在当前数据上的改进）、潜力（数据集驱动的性能变化）、保留（跨修改步骤的知识保存），通过这三个维度解耦性能变化来源

Result: 模拟人口分布变化的案例研究表明：渐进过渡可实现稳定的学习和保留，而快速变化则揭示可塑性与稳定性之间的权衡；这些测量为监管科学提供了实用见解

Conclusion: 提出的三指标方法能够严格评估自适应AI系统在连续修改过程中的安全性和有效性，为医疗设备自适应AI的监管评估提供了实用框架

Abstract: This work addresses challenges in evaluating adaptive artificial intelligence (AI) models for medical devices, where iterative updates to both models and evaluation datasets complicate performance assessment. We introduce a novel approach with three complementary measurements: learning (model improvement on current data), potential (dataset-driven performance shifts), and retention (knowledge preservation across modification steps), to disentangle performance changes caused by model adaptations versus dynamic environments. Case studies using simulated population shifts demonstrate the approach's utility: gradual transitions enable stable learning and retention, while rapid shifts reveal trade-offs between plasticity and stability. These measurements provide practical insights for regulatory science, enabling rigorous assessment of the safety and effectiveness of adaptive AI systems over sequential modifications.

</details>


### [197] [QED-Nano: Teaching a Tiny Model to Prove Hard Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04898)
*LM-Provers,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Jasper Dekoninck,Edward Beeching,Jia Li,Ian Wu,Lewis Tunstall,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QED-Nano是一个4B参数的开放模型，通过三阶段训练方法在奥林匹克数学证明问题上达到接近专有模型的性能，同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有AI系统在复杂数学证明问题上表现出色，但其训练流程不透明、依赖大型内部模型和脚手架，导致运行昂贵、难以复现和研究。本文旨在探索小型开放模型是否也能在奥林匹克数学证明问题上达到竞争性推理性能。

Method: 采用三阶段训练方法：1) 从DeepSeek-Math-V2蒸馏进行监督微调，学习良好证明写作风格；2) 基于评分标准的强化学习；3) 扩展强化学习，使用推理缓存将长证明分解为迭代的总结-精炼循环，增强测试时推理能力。

Result: QED-Nano在证明生成性能上超越了包括Nomos-1和GPT-OSS-120B在内的更大开放模型，接近Gemini 3 Pro等专有模型的性能，同时推理成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 小型开放模型通过精心设计的训练方法可以在奥林匹克数学证明问题上达到竞争性性能，为开放数学推理研究提供了可行路径。作者发布了完整的QED-Nano管道以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Proprietary AI systems have recently demonstrated impressive capabilities on complex proof-based problems, with gold-level performance reported at the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO). However, the training pipelines behind these systems remain largely undisclosed, and their reliance on large "internal" models and scaffolds makes them expensive to run, difficult to reproduce, and hard to study or improve upon. This raises a central question: can small, open models also be trained to achieve competitive reasoning performance on difficult Olympiad-level math? In this paper, we answer this question by building QED-Nano, a 4B model post-trained for Olympiad-level proofs. Our training recipe has three stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning to imbue good proof-writing styles by distilling from DeepSeek-Math-V2, (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with rubric-based rewards, and (3) expanding RL with a reasoning cache, which decomposes long proofs into iterative summarize-and-refine cycles and enables stronger test-time reasoning. QED-Nano surpasses the proof-generation performance of much larger open models, including Nomos-1 and GPT-OSS-120B, and approaches the performance of proprietary models like Gemini 3 Pro, at a fraction of the inference cost. To support further research on open mathematical reasoning, we release the full QED-Nano pipeline, including the QED-Nano and QED-Nano-SFT models, the FineProofs-SFT and FineProofs-RL datasets, and the training and evaluation code.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Learning-Based Fault Detection for Legged Robots in Remote Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03397)
*Abriana Stewart-Height,Seema Jahagirdar,Nikolai Matni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发基于离线学习的四足机器人单肢故障检测方法，通过本体感受器数据识别肢体损伤，为控制器选择合适的三足步态提供依据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险环境中，四足机器人无法像人类和动物那样自然识别肢体损伤并调整运动方式。机器人自主执行远程任务时，检测肢体损伤并适应新物理形态的能力关乎生存与失效

Method: 采用基于离线学习的方法，利用四足机器人的本体感受器数据来检测单肢故障。故障检测技术旨在为控制器提供正确输出，使其能根据机器人当前物理形态选择合适的三足步态

Result: 开发并实现了单肢故障检测系统，能够从本体感受器数据中识别肢体损伤，为后续步态调整提供决策依据

Conclusion: 提出的离线学习故障检测方法使四足机器人能够在肢体损伤时自主调整运动策略，提高了在动态复杂环境中执行任务时的生存能力和适应性

Abstract: Operations in hazardous environments put humans, animals, and machines at high risk for physically damaging consequences. In contrast to humans and animals, quadruped robots cannot naturally identify and adjust their locomotion to a severely debilitated limb. The ability to detect limb damage and adjust movement to a new physical morphology is the difference between survival and death for humans and animals. The same can be said for quadruped robots autonomously carrying out remote assignments in dynamic, complex settings. This work presents the development and implementation of an off-line learning-based method to detect single limb faults from proprioceptive sensor data in a quadrupedal robot. The aim of the fault detection technique is to provide the correct output for the controller to select the appropriate tripedal gait to use given the robot's current physical morphology.

</details>


### [199] [Diffusion Policy with Bayesian Expert Selection for Active Multi-Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03404)
*Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Yaakov Bar-Shalom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯框架用于扩散策略的专家选择，通过变分贝叶斯最后一层模型预测各专家策略的跟踪性能及不确定性，基于悲观原则选择最坏情况下表现最佳的专家，提升多目标主动跟踪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略通过去噪过程隐式选择行为策略，缺乏对执行策略的不确定性量化。主动多目标跟踪需要在探索未检测目标和利用不确定跟踪目标之间平衡，需要显式的、不确定性感知的策略选择机制。

Method: 将专家选择建模为离线上下文赌博机问题，提出贝叶斯框架：1) 使用多头变分贝叶斯最后一层模型预测给定当前信念状态下各专家策略的期望跟踪性能及预测不确定性；2) 基于离线决策的悲观原则，采用下界置信准则选择最坏情况下预测性能最佳的专家；3) 所选专家条件化扩散策略生成相应动作序列。

Result: 在模拟室内跟踪场景实验中，该方法优于基础扩散策略和标准门控方法，包括混合专家选择和确定性回归基线。

Conclusion: 提出的贝叶斯框架为扩散策略提供了不确定性感知的专家选择机制，通过悲观原则避免对不可靠预测的过度承诺，在主动多目标跟踪任务中实现了更好的性能平衡。

Abstract: Active multi-target tracking requires a mobile robot to balance exploration for undetected targets with exploitation of uncertain tracked ones. Diffusion policies have emerged as a powerful approach for capturing diverse behavioral strategies by learning action sequences from expert demonstrations. However, existing methods implicitly select among strategies through the denoising process, without uncertainty quantification over which strategy to execute. We formulate expert selection for diffusion policies as an offline contextual bandit problem and propose a Bayesian framework for pessimistic, uncertainty-aware strategy selection. A multi-head Variational Bayesian Last Layer (VBLL) model predicts the expected tracking performance of each expert strategy given the current belief state, providing both a point estimate and predictive uncertainty. Following the pessimism principle for offline decision-making, a Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) criterion then selects the expert whose worst-case predicted performance is best, avoiding overcommitment to experts with unreliable predictions. The selected expert conditions a diffusion policy to generate corresponding action sequences. Experiments on simulated indoor tracking scenarios demonstrate that our approach outperforms both the base diffusion policy and standard gating methods, including Mixture-of-Experts selection and deterministic regression baselines.

</details>


### [200] [Do Robots Need Body Language? Comparing Communication Modalities for Legible Motion Intent in Human-Shared Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03451)
*Jonathan Albert Cohen,Kye Shimizu,Allen Song,Vishnu Bharath,Kent Larson,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究评估了四足机器人（波士顿动力Spot）在不同信号模态（表达性运动、灯光、文本、音频）下对人类理解其导航意图的影响，比较了隐式与显式信号策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在共享空间中常以难以理解的方式移动，需要人类适应。高自由度机器人的运动具有表达性，无论是否有意，都会传递信号，因此需要理解这些线索如何被感知。

Method: 通过在线视频研究，在四种常见场景中评估不同信号模态（表达性运动、灯光、文本、音频）如何影响人类：（1）预测机器人下一步导航动作的准确性，（2）预测信心，（3）对机器人安全行为的信任。测试了表达性运动与显式通道的比较、对齐多模态线索是否增强可解释性、以及冲突线索如何影响用户信心和信任。

Result: 提供了关于隐式与显式信号策略相对有效性的初步证据，具体测量了不同模态对预测准确性、信心和信任的影响。

Conclusion: 研究为理解机器人信号模态对人类可解释性的影响提供了基础，有助于设计更有效的机器人-人类交互策略。

Abstract: Robots in shared spaces often move in ways that are difficult for people to interpret, placing the burden on humans to adapt. High-DoF robots exhibit motion that people read as expressive, intentionally or not, making it important to understand how such cues are perceived. We present an online video study evaluating how different signaling modalities, expressive motion, lights, text, and audio, shape people's ability to understand a quadruped robot's upcoming navigation actions (Boston Dynamics Spot). Across four common scenarios, we measure how each modality influences humans' (1) accuracy in predicting the robot's next navigation action, (2) confidence in that prediction, and (3) trust in the robot to act safely. The study tests how expressive motions compare to explicit channels, whether aligned multimodal cues enhance interpretability, and how conflicting cues affect user confidence and trust. We contribute initial evidence on the relative effectiveness of implicit versus explicit signaling strategies.

</details>


### [201] [Sim2Real-AD: A Modular Sim-to-Real Framework for Deploying VLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning in Real-World Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03497)
*Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Zihao Sheng,Boyue Wang,Junwei You,Yue Leng,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim2Real-AD：一个模块化框架，实现CARLA训练的VLM引导RL策略到真实全尺寸车辆的零样本仿真到现实迁移，无需真实世界RL训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在仿真中训练的VLM引导RL策略部署到真实自动驾驶车辆时的根本挑战，特别是仿真原生观测和仿真耦合动作语义在物理平台上不可用的问题。

Method: 框架包含四个组件：几何观测桥（GOB）将单目前视图转换为BEV观测；物理感知动作映射（PAM）将策略输出转换为平台无关的物理命令；两阶段渐进训练（TPT）策略分离动作空间和观测空间迁移以稳定适应；实时部署管道（RDP）集成感知、策略推理、控制转换和安全监控。

Result: 仿真实验显示框架保持了不同奖励范式下代表性RL算法的相对性能排序，并验证了每个模块的贡献。在全尺寸福特E-Transit上的零样本部署在跟车、避障和停车标志交互场景中分别达到90%、80%和75%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究首次展示了CARLA训练的VLM引导RL策略在全尺寸真实车辆上的零样本闭环部署，无需任何真实世界RL训练数据，为仿真到现实迁移提供了有效的模块化解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying reinforcement learning policies trained in simulation to real autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge, particularly for VLM-guided RL frameworks whose policies are typically learned with simulator-native observations and simulator-coupled action semantics that are unavailable on physical platforms. This paper presents Sim2Real-AD, a modular framework for zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of CARLA-trained VLM-guided RL policies to full-scale vehicles without any real-world RL training data. The framework decomposes the transfer problem into four components: a Geometric Observation Bridge (GOB) that converts monocular front-view images into simulator-compatible bird's-eye-view (BEV) observations, a Physics-Aware Action Mapping (PAM) that translates policy outputs into platform-agnostic physical commands, a Two-Phase Progressive Training (TPT) strategy that stabilizes adaptation by separating action-space and observation-space transfer, and a Real-time Deployment Pipeline (RDP) that integrates perception, policy inference, control conversion, and safety monitoring for closed-loop execution. Simulation experiments show that the framework preserves the relative performance ordering of representative RL algorithms across different reward paradigms and validate the contribution of each module. Zero-shot deployment on a full-scale Ford E-Transit achieves success rates of 90%, 80%, and 75% in car-following, obstacle avoidance, and stop-sign interaction scenarios, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this study is among the first to demonstrate zero-shot closed-loop deployment of a CARLA-trained VLM-guided RL policy on a full-scale real vehicle without any real-world RL training data. The demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/Sim2Real-AD-website/.

</details>


### [202] [CRAFT: Video Diffusion for Bimanual Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03552)
*Jason Chen,I-Chun Arthur Liu,Gaurav Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个基于视频扩散的框架，通过边缘引导从模拟轨迹生成逼真的双手操作演示视频和动作标签，显著扩展训练数据多样性和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界双手机器人演示数据成本高、视觉多样性有限，限制了策略在不同视角、物体配置和机器人本体间的鲁棒性，需要可扩展的数据生成方法

Method: 基于视频扩散变换器，利用模拟轨迹提取的边缘结构线索作为条件，生成时序连贯的操纵视频并产生动作标签，支持物体姿态变化、相机视角、光照背景变化、跨本体迁移和多视角合成等统一增强流程

Result: 在模拟和真实世界双手任务中，CRAFT相比现有增强策略和简单数据扩展显著提高了成功率，证明扩散视频生成能大幅扩展演示多样性并改善双臂操作任务的泛化能力

Conclusion: CRAFT框架通过边缘引导的视频扩散生成，能够从少量真实演示出发创建大规模、视觉多样的逼真训练数据，绕过真实机器人重放演示的需求，为双手操作学习提供了可扩展的数据生成解决方案

Abstract: Bimanual robot learning from demonstrations is fundamentally limited by the cost and narrow visual diversity of real-world data, which constrains policy robustness across viewpoints, object configurations, and embodiments. We present Canny-guided Robot Data Generation using Video Diffusion Transformers (CRAFT), a video diffusion-based framework for scalable bimanual demonstration generation that synthesizes temporally coherent manipulation videos while producing action labels. By conditioning video diffusion on edge-based structural cues extracted from simulator-generated trajectories, CRAFT produces physically plausible trajectory variations and supports a unified augmentation pipeline spanning object pose changes, camera viewpoints, lighting and background variations, cross-embodiment transfer, and multi-view synthesis. We leverage a pre-trained video diffusion model to convert simulated videos, along with action labels from the simulation trajectories, into action-consistent demonstrations. Starting from only a few real-world demonstrations, CRAFT generates a large, visually diverse set of photorealistic training data, bypassing the need to replay demonstrations on the real robot (Sim2Real). Across simulated and real-world bimanual tasks, CRAFT improves success rates over existing augmentation strategies and straightforward data scaling, demonstrating that diffusion-based video generation can substantially expand demonstration diversity and improve generalization for dual-arm manipulation tasks. Our project website is available at: https://craftaug.github.io/

</details>


### [203] [HAD: Combining Hierarchical Diffusion with Metric-Decoupled RL for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03581)
*Wenhao Yao,Xinglong Sun,Zhenxin Li,Shiyi Lan,Zi Wang,Jose M. Alvarez,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HAD是一个端到端自动驾驶规划框架，采用分层扩散策略，通过结构保持轨迹扩展生成现实轨迹，并使用度量解耦策略优化进行多目标强化学习训练，在NAVSIM和HUGSIM基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶规划面临两个主要挑战：1）基于评分-选择框架的模型直接从整个候选空间选择轨迹难以优化，且扩散模型的高斯扰动常产生不现实的轨迹；2）强化学习训练通常使用单一耦合奖励，缺乏结构化信号，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出HAD框架，包含三个核心组件：1）分层扩散策略，将规划分解为从粗到细的过程；2）结构保持轨迹扩展，生成保持运动学结构的现实轨迹候选；3）度量解耦策略优化，实现跨多个驾驶目标的结构化强化学习优化。

Result: 在NAVSIM和HUGSIM基准测试中，HAD取得了新的最先进性能：在NAVSIM上EPDMS指标提升+2.3，在HUGSIM上路线完成率提升+4.9，大幅超越先前方法。

Conclusion: HAD通过分层扩散策略、结构保持轨迹扩展和度量解耦策略优化，有效解决了端到端自动驾驶规划中的轨迹生成和强化学习优化难题，在多个基准上展现了卓越性能。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has emerged as a dominant paradigm for autonomous driving, where recent models often adopt a scoring-selection framework to choose trajectories from a large set of candidates, with diffusion-based decoding showing strong promise. However, directly selecting from the entire candidate space remains difficult to optimize, and Gaussian perturbations used in diffusion often introduce unrealistic trajectories that complicate the denoising process. In addition, for training these models, reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise, but existing end-to-end RL approaches typically rely on a single coupled reward without structured signals, limiting optimization effectiveness. To address these challenges, we propose HAD, an end-to-end planning framework with a Hierarchical Diffusion Policy that decomposes planning into a coarse-to-fine process. To improve trajectory generation, we introduce Structure-Preserved Trajectory Expansion, which produces realistic candidates while maintaining kinematic structure. For policy learning, we develop Metric-Decoupled Policy Optimization (MDPO) to enable structured RL optimization across multiple driving objectives. Extensive experiments show that HAD achieves new state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM and HUGSIM, outperforming prior arts by a huge margin: +2.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM and +4.9 Route Completion on HUGSIM.

</details>


### [204] [Human-Robot Copilot for Data-Efficient Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03613)
*Rui Yan,Zaitian Gongye,Lars Paulsen,Xuxin Cheng,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human-Robot Copilot框架，通过可调节的缩放因子实现灵巧遥操作，同时保持与多种机械臂的兼容性，在有限演示数据下提高策略性能并减少干预需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式模仿学习或人在回路方法在平衡灵巧性和通用性方面存在局限：要么提供精细校正但仅限于特定运动学结构，要么实现通用性但牺牲精确控制。需要一种既能实现灵巧遥操作又能兼容多种机械臂的解决方案。

Method: 提出Human-Robot Copilot框架，通过可调节的缩放因子实现灵巧遥操作，同时保持与多种工业和研究用机械臂的兼容性。该框架基于Human-Gated DAgger范式，通过选择性人类干预增强演示数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，在相同数量的演示轨迹下，该框架实现了更高的性能。由于只需间歇性进行校正干预，整体数据收集过程更加高效且耗时更少。

Conclusion: Human-Robot Copilot框架成功解决了现有方法在灵巧性和通用性之间的权衡问题，通过可调节缩放因子实现了既灵巧又通用的遥操作系统，提高了有限演示数据下的策略性能和数据收集效率。

Abstract: Collecting human demonstrations via teleoperation is a common approach for teaching robots task-specific skills. However, when only a limited number of demonstrations are available, policies are prone to entering out-of-distribution (OOD) states due to compounding errors or environmental stochasticity. Existing interactive imitation learning or human-in-the-loop methods try to address this issue by following the Human-Gated DAgger (HG-DAgger) paradigm, an approach that augments demonstrations through selective human intervention during policy execution. Nevertheless, these approaches struggle to balance dexterity and generality: they either provide fine-grained corrections but are limited to specific kinematic structures, or achieve generality at the cost of precise control. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Human-Robot Copilot framework that can leverage a scaling factor for dexterous teleoperation while maintaining compatibility with a wide range of industrial and research manipulators. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework achieves higher performance with the same number of demonstration trajectories. Moreover, since corrective interventions are required only intermittently, the overall data collection process is more efficient and less time-consuming.

</details>


### [205] [Towards Edge Intelligence via Autonomous Navigation: A Robot-Assisted Data Collection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03623)
*Tingting Huang,Yingyang Chen,Sixian Qin,Zhijian Lin,Jun Li,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通信与学习双驱动(CLD)自主导航方案，用于移动机器人在复杂NLoS环境中的数据采集，通过区域感知传播特性和非点质量机器人表示，同时优化导航、通信和学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统对大规模高质量数据的需求日益增长，移动机器人被部署在复杂环境中主动采集数据。现有机器人辅助数据采集方法在非视距(NLoS)环境中难以实现可靠高效性能。

Method: 提出通信与学习双驱动(CLD)自主导航方案，结合区域感知传播特性和非点质量机器人表示；基于主化最小化(MM)算法解决非凸非光滑的CLD优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案在避障导航、数据采集和模型训练方面优于基准方法；CLD能通过灵活调整导航、通信和学习目标的权重因子适应不同场景。

Conclusion: CLD方案能有效解决NLoS环境中机器人辅助数据采集的挑战，实现导航、通信和学习性能的协同优化，为边缘智能系统提供可靠高效的数据采集解决方案。

Abstract: With the growing demand for large-scale and high-quality data in edge intelligence systems, mobile robots are increasingly deployed to collect data proactively, particularly in complex environments. However, existing robot-assisted data collection methods face significant challenges in achieving reliable and efficient performance, especially in non-line-of-sight (NLoS) environments. This paper proposes a communication-and-learning dual-driven (CLD) autonomous navigation scheme that incorporates region-aware propagation characteristics and a non-point-mass robot representation. This scheme enables simultaneous optimization of navigation, communication, and learning performance. An efficient algorithm based on majorization-minimization (MM) is proposed to solve the non-convex and non-smooth CLD problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves superior performance in collision-avoidance navigation, data collection, and model training compared to benchmark methods. It is also shown that CLD can adapt to different scenarios by flexibly adjusting the weight factor among navigation, communication and learning objectives.

</details>


### [206] [A Multi-View 3D Telepresence System for XR Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03730)
*Enes Ulas Dincer,Manuel Zaremski,Alexandra Nick,Elias Wucher,Barbara Deml,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合多视角几何点云与手腕RGB流的多视图VR遥操作系统，在Quest 3上实现实时渲染，通过用户实验验证其在机器人遥操作任务中的优越性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统屏幕界面在机器人遥操作中缺乏直观的3D可视化与可靠深度线索，而现有VR遥操作系统在全局3D结构与局部高分辨率细节之间存在权衡，需要一种能同时提供两者优势的解决方案

Method: 开发多视图VR临场感系统：1) 融合三个摄像头的几何信息，在独立VR硬件上实现GPU加速的点云渲染；2) 集成手腕安装的RGB流，在点云精度有限区域提供高分辨率局部细节；系统在Meta Quest 3上支持约75k点的实时渲染

Result: 31名参与者的组内研究显示，在三种遥操作任务中，该系统在任务成功率、完成时间、感知工作负荷和可用性方面表现最佳；纯点云模式也优于RGB流和OpenTeleVision，但融合系统整体性能最优

Conclusion: 结合全局3D结构与局部高分辨率细节能显著提升遥操作临场感，为下一代机器人遥操作系统提供了坚实基础，证明了多视图融合方法的有效性

Abstract: Robot teleoperation is critical for applications such as remote maintenance, fleet robotics, search and rescue, and data collection for robot learning. Effective teleoperation requires intuitive 3D visualization with reliable depth cues, which conventional screen-based interfaces often fail to provide. We introduce a multi-view VR telepresence system that (1) fuses geometry from three cameras to produce GPU-accelerated point-cloud rendering on standalone VR hardware, and (2) integrates a wrist-mounted RGB stream to provide high-resolution local detail where point-cloud accuracy is limited. Our pipeline supports real-time rendering of approximately 75k points on the Meta Quest 3. A within-subject study was conducted with 31 participants to compare our system to other visualisation modalities, such as RGB streams, a projection of stereo-vision directly in the VR device and point clouds without providing additional RGB information. Across three different teleoperated manipulation tasks, we measured task success, completion time, perceived workload, and usability. Our system achieved the best overall performance, while the Point Cloud modality without RGB also outperforming the RGB streams and OpenTeleVision. These results show that combining global 3D structure with localized high-resolution detail substantially improves telepresence for manipulation and provides a strong foundation for next-generation robot teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [207] [Build on Priors: Vision--Language--Guided Neuro-Symbolic Imitation Learning for Data-Efficient Real-World Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03759)
*Pierrick Lorang,Johannes Huemer,Timothy Duggan,Kai Goebel,Patrik Zips,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从少量演示中自动构建符号规划域和数据高效控制策略的神经符号框架，无需人工领域工程，在工业叉车和机械臂上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法依赖手工符号抽象、语义标注轨迹或大量演示数据，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。需要一种能从少量无标注演示中自动学习长时程操作任务的框架。

Method: 1) 分割演示为技能；2) 使用视觉语言模型(VLM)分类技能并识别等价高层状态，构建状态转移图；3) 用ASP求解器合成PDDL规划域；4) 利用oracle函数为每个技能策略提取最小化任务相关观察和动作空间；5) 在控制参考层面而非原始执行器层面学习策略；6) 利用已知控制器进行真实世界数据增强。

Result: 在真实工业叉车上进行统计严谨的操作试验验证，并在Kinova Gen3机械臂上展示跨平台通用性。框架实现了可扩展、数据高效、无需专家且可解释的神经符号机器人学习。

Conclusion: 将控制学习、VLM驱动的抽象和自动规划合成统一到一个流程中，为可扩展、数据高效、无需专家且可解释的神经符号机器人提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Enabling robots to learn long-horizon manipulation tasks from a handful of demonstrations remains a central challenge in robotics. Existing neuro-symbolic approaches often rely on hand-crafted symbolic abstractions, semantically labeled trajectories or large demonstration datasets, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present a scalable neuro-symbolic framework that autonomously constructs symbolic planning domains and data-efficient control policies from as few as one to thirty unannotated skill demonstrations, without requiring manual domain engineering. Our method segments demonstrations into skills and employs a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to classify skills and identify equivalent high-level states, enabling automatic construction of a state-transition graph. This graph is processed by an Answer Set Programming solver to synthesize a PDDL planning domain, which an oracle function exploits to isolate the minimal, task-relevant and target relative observation and action spaces for each skill policy. Policies are learned at the control reference level rather than at the raw actuator signal level, yielding a smoother and less noisy learning target. Known controllers can be leveraged for real-world data augmentation by projecting a single demonstration onto other objects in the scene, simultaneously enriching the graph construction process and the dataset for imitation learning. We validate our framework primarily on a real industrial forklift across statistically rigorous manipulation trials, and demonstrate cross-platform generality on a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm across two standard benchmarks. Our results show that grounding control learning, VLM-driven abstraction, and automated planning synthesis into a unified pipeline constitutes a practical path toward scalable, data-efficient, expert-free and interpretable neuro-symbolic robotics.

</details>


### [208] [A Novel Hybrid PID-LQR Controller for Sit-To-Stand Assistance Using a CAD-Integrated Simscape Multibody Lower Limb Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03766)
*Ranjeet Kumbhar,Rajmeet Singh,Appaso M Gadade,Ashish Singla,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于下肢外骨骼坐-站转换的混合PID-LQR控制器，相比传统PID和LQR控制器，在轨迹跟踪精度、响应速度和稳定性方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐-站转换过程中，人-外骨骼系统的高度非线性、时变动力学特性以及临床安全对轨迹跟踪的严格要求，使得精确控制成为康复机器人领域的核心挑战。

Method: 1) 从SolidWorks CAD装配体导入MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody构建高保真物理动力学模型；2) 使用OpenSim生成生理学参考关节轨迹并分解为三个生物力学阶段；3) 提出混合PID-LQR控制器，通过调谐混合系数α=0.65结合LQR的最优瞬态响应和PID的积分扰动抑制能力。

Result: 混合PID-LQR控制器相比PID在髋关节和膝关节的RMSE分别降低72.3%和70.4%，所有关节的稳定时间减少90%以上，超调量限制在2.39%-6.10%范围内，在所有评估性能指标上均优于基线策略。

Conclusion: 混合PID-LQR控制器在坐-站转换任务中表现出卓越性能，验证了其在临床辅助外骨骼部署中的强大转化潜力，为康复机器人控制提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Precise control of lower limb exoskeletons during sit-to-stand (STS) transitions remains a central challenge in rehabilitation robotics owing to the highly nonlinear, time-varying dynamics of the human-exoskeleton system and the stringent trajectory tracking requirements imposed by clinical safety. This paper presents the systematic design, simulation, and comparative evaluation of three control strategies: a classical Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller, a Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR), and a novel Hybrid PID-LQR controller applied to a bilateral lower limb exoskeleton performing the sit-to-stand transition. A high-fidelity, physics-based dynamic model of the exoskeleton is constructed by importing a SolidWorks CAD assembly directly into the MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody environment, preserving accurate geometric and inertial properties of all links. Physiologically representative reference joint trajectories for the hip, knee, and ankle joints are generated using OpenSim musculoskeletal simulation and decomposed into three biomechanical phases: flexion-momentum (0-33%), momentum-transfer (34-66%), and extension (67-100%). The proposed Hybrid PID-LQR controller combines the optimal transient response of LQR with the integral disturbance rejection of PID through a tuned blending coefficient alpha = 0.65. Simulation results demonstrate that the Hybrid PID-LQR achieves RMSE reductions of 72.3% and 70.4% over PID at the hip and knee joints, respectively, reduces settling time by over 90% relative to PID across all joints, and limits overshoot to 2.39%-6.10%, confirming its superiority over both baseline strategies across all evaluated performance metrics and demonstrating strong translational potential for clinical assistive exoskeleton deployment.

</details>


### [209] [OpenRC: An Open-Source Robotic Colonoscopy Framework for Multimodal Data Acquisition and Autonomy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03781)
*Siddhartha Kapuria,Mohammad Rafiee Javazm,Naruhiko Ikoma,Joga Ivatury,Mohammad Ali Nasseri,Nassir Navab,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRC是一个开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架，通过改造传统结肠镜同时保留临床工作流程，支持多模态数据同步采集，为机器人结肠镜和手术自主性研究提供可重复基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结肠镜平台在系统研究操作者控制、器械运动和视觉反馈的耦合动态方面支持有限，限制了机器人结肠镜、医学影像和新兴视觉-语言-动作学习范式的可重复闭环研究。

Method: 开发开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架OpenRC，改造传统结肠镜同时保留临床工作流程，支持视频、操作者指令、驱动状态和远端尖端姿态的同步记录，并验证运动一致性和跨模态延迟。

Result: 收集了包含1,894个远程操作片段（约19小时）的多模态数据集，涵盖10种结构化任务变体，包括常规导航、失败事件和恢复行为；验证了运动一致性并量化了跨模态延迟。

Conclusion: OpenRC通过统一开放硬件和对齐的多模态数据集，为多模态机器人结肠镜和手术自主性研究提供了可重复的基础平台。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer screening critically depends on colonoscopy, yet existing platforms offer limited support for systematically studying the coupled dynamics of operator control, instrument motion, and visual feedback. This gap restricts reproducible closed-loop research in robotic colonoscopy, medical imaging, and emerging vision-language-action (VLA) learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we present OpenRC, an open-source modular robotic colonoscopy framework that retrofits conventional scopes while preserving clinical workflow. The framework supports simultaneous recording of video, operator commands, actuation state, and distal tip pose. We experimentally validated motion consistency and quantified cross-modal latency across sensing streams. Using this platform, we collected a multimodal dataset comprising 1,894 teleoperated episodes ~19 hours across 10 structured task variations of routine navigation, failure events, and recovery behaviors. By unifying open hardware and an aligned multimodal dataset, OpenRC provides a reproducible foundation for research in multimodal robotic colonoscopy and surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [210] [DC-Ada: Reward-Only Decentralized Observation-Interface Adaptation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03905)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-Ada是一种仅使用奖励信号的去中心化适应方法，通过调整每个机器人的观测变换来适应异构传感器，而不修改预训练的共享策略，在严格步数预算下使用基于接受/拒绝的随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构性是部署多机器人团队的一个关键特征：平台通常在感知模态、范围、视野和故障模式上存在差异。在标称感知下训练的控制器在部署到传感器缺失或不匹配的机器人上时性能会急剧下降，即使任务和动作接口保持不变。

Method: DC-Ada保持预训练的共享策略冻结，仅调整紧凑的每机器人观测变换，将异构感知映射到固定的推理接口。该方法是无梯度的、通信最小化的：使用预算化的接受/拒绝随机搜索，在严格步数预算下进行短期的公共随机数滚动。

Result: 在2D多机器人模拟器中评估了四个异构机制（H0-H3）和五个种子，预算为200,000个联合环境步数。结果显示异构性会显著降低冻结共享策略的性能，没有单一缓解方法在所有任务和指标上占优。DC-Ada在严重覆盖制图任务中明显提高了完成率，仅需要标量团队回报，无需策略微调或持久通信。

Conclusion: DC-Ada为异构团队提供了一个实用的部署时适应方法，作为现有方法的补充操作点，在保持策略不变的情况下通过观测变换适应传感器异构性。

Abstract: Heterogeneity is a defining feature of deployed multi-robot teams: platforms often differ in sensing modalities, ranges, fields of view, and failure patterns. Controllers trained under nominal sensing can degrade sharply when deployed on robots with missing or mismatched sensors, even when the task and action interface are unchanged. We present DC-Ada, a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that keeps a pretrained shared policy frozen and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to map heterogeneous sensing into a fixed inference interface. DC-Ada is gradient-free and communication-minimal: it uses budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under a strict step budget. We evaluate DC-Ada against four baselines in a deterministic 2D multi-robot simulator covering warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, across four heterogeneity regimes (H0--H3) and five seeds with a matched budget of $200{,}000$ joint environment steps per run. Results show that heterogeneity can substantially degrade a frozen shared policy and that no single mitigation dominates across all tasks and metrics. Observation normalization is strongest for reward robustness in warehouse logistics and competitive in search and rescue, while the frozen shared policy is strongest for reward in collaborative mapping. DC-Ada offers a useful complementary operating point: it improves completion most clearly in severe coverage-based mapping while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication. These results position DC-Ada as a practical deploy-time adaptation method for heterogeneous teams.

</details>


### [211] [VA-FastNavi-MARL: Real-Time Robot Control with Multimedia-Driven Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03998)
*Yang Zhang,Shengxi Jing,Fengxiang Wang,Yuan Feng,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VA-FastNavi-MARL：基于元强化学习的异步视听对齐框架，实现实时多模态人机交互导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态异构多媒体指令的实时响应问题，克服传统方法因繁重感官处理导致的延迟瓶颈，实现低延迟、鲁棒的人机交互控制

Method: 提出VA-FastNavi-MARL框架，将异步视听输入对齐到统一潜在表示空间，通过元强化学习将多样化指令建模为可导航目标分布，实现对新指令的快速适应

Result: 在多臂工作空间验证中显著优于基线方法，在样本效率和实时执行方面表现优异，即使在噪声多媒体流下仍保持鲁棒性

Conclusion: 该框架通过模态无关的流处理实现了无缝低延迟控制，为实时人机交互中的多模态指令理解提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Interpreting dynamic, heterogeneous multimedia commands with real-time responsiveness is critical for Human-Robot Interaction. We present VA-FastNavi-MARL, a framework that aligns asynchronous audio-visual inputs into a unified latent representation. By treating diverse instructions as a distribution of navigable goals via Meta-Reinforcement Learning, our method enables rapid adaptation to unseen directives with negligible inference overhead. Unlike approaches bottlenecked by heavy sensory processing, our modality-agnostic stream ensures seamless, low-latency control. Validation on a multi-arm workspace confirms that VA-FastNavi-MARL significantly outperforms baselines in sample efficiency and maintains robust, real-time execution even under noisy multimedia streams.

</details>


### [212] [Dynamic Whole-Body Dancing with Humanoid Robots -- A Model-Based Control Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03999)
*Shibowen Zhang,Jiayang Wu,Guannan Liu,Helin Zhu,Junjie Liu,Zhehan Li,Junhong Guo,Xiaokun Leng,Hangxin Liu,Jingwen Zhang,Jikai Wang,Zonghai Chen,Zhicheng He,Jiayi Wang,Yao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的集成框架，用于人形机器人动态全身舞蹈动作的生成与执行，包含离线动作生成和在线动作执行两阶段，通过未来状态预测实现鲁棒动态舞蹈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为人形机器人开发能够在现实环境中执行动态、鲁棒舞蹈动作的框架，解决从人类演示到机器人执行的全流程问题，提高动作表现力和稳定性

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 离线阶段：通过MoCap捕捉人类舞蹈演示，QP问题重定向到机器人，轨迹优化确保动态可行性；2) 在线阶段：基于质心动力的MPC框架实时跟踪规划动作，主动调整摆动脚位置以适应扰动

Result: 在Kuavo 4Pro全尺寸人形机器人上验证，在仿真和4分钟现场公开表演中展示动态舞蹈动作，实验表明更长的预测时域能提高规划中的动作表现力和执行中的稳定性

Conclusion: 提出的集成框架成功实现了人形机器人的动态舞蹈动作生成与执行，通过未来状态预测和两阶段处理，在现实环境中表现出鲁棒性和动态性，为复杂动态任务提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated model-based framework for generating and executing dynamic whole-body dance motions on humanoid robots. The framework operates in two stages: offline motion generation and online motion execution, both leveraging future state prediction to enable robust and dynamic dance motions in real-world environments. In the offline motion generation stage, human dance demonstrations are captured via a motion capture (MoCap) system, retargeted to the robot by solving a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, and further refined using Trajectory Optimization (TO) to ensure dynamic feasibility. In the online motion execution stage, a centroidal dynamics-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework tracks the planned motions in real time and proactively adjusts swing foot placement to adapt to real world disturbances. We validate our framework on the full-size humanoid robot Kuavo 4Pro, demonstrating the dynamic dance motions both in simulation and in a four-minute live public performance with a team of four robots. Experimental results show that longer prediction horizons improve both motion expressiveness in planning and stability in execution.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Dexterous Grasping from Sparse Taxonomy Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04138)
*Juhan Park,Taerim Yoon,Seungmin Kim,Joonggil Kim,Wontae Ye,Jeongeun Park,Yoonbyung Chai,Geonwoo Cho,Geunwoo Cho,Dohyeong Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Yongjae Kim,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRIT是一个两阶段框架，通过稀疏分类学指导学习灵巧控制，先预测基于分类学的抓取规范，再基于此生成连续手指动作，在保持抓取结构的同时完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要为每个物体和任务规划合适的抓取配置，但为每个对象和任务指定密集的位姿或接触目标不切实际。同时，仅从任务奖励进行端到端强化学习缺乏可控性，难以在失败时进行干预。

Method: GRIT采用两阶段框架：第一阶段从场景和任务上下文预测基于分类学的稀疏抓取规范；第二阶段基于这个稀疏指令，策略生成连续的手指动作，在完成任务的同时保持预期的抓取结构。

Result: 结果显示某些抓取分类学对特定物体几何更有效。利用这种关系，GRIT相比基线方法提高了对新物体的泛化能力，总体成功率达到87.9%。真实世界实验展示了可控性，能够基于物体几何和任务意图通过高级分类学选择调整抓取策略。

Conclusion: GRIT框架通过稀疏分类学指导实现了可控的灵巧操作学习，在保持任务性能的同时提供了用户干预的能力，为实际应用中的灵巧操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires planning a grasp configuration suited to the object and task, which is then executed through coordinated multi-finger control. However, specifying grasp plans with dense pose or contact targets for every object and task is impractical. Meanwhile, end-to-end reinforcement learning from task rewards alone lacks controllability, making it difficult for users to intervene when failures occur. To this end, we present GRIT, a two-stage framework that learns dexterous control from sparse taxonomy guidance. GRIT first predicts a taxonomy-based grasp specification from the scene and task context. Conditioned on this sparse command, a policy generates continuous finger motions that accomplish the task while preserving the intended grasp structure. Our result shows that certain grasp taxonomies are more effective for specific object geometries. By leveraging this relationship, GRIT improves generalization to novel objects over baselines and achieves an overall success rate of 87.9%. Moreover, real-world experiments demonstrate controllability, enabling grasp strategies to be adjusted through high-level taxonomy selection based on object geometry and task intent.

</details>


### [214] [Adaptive Action Chunking at Inference-time for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04161)
*Yuanchang Liang,Xiaobo Wang,Kai Wang,Shuo Wang,Xiaojiang Peng,Haoyu Chen,David Kim Huat Chua,Prahlad Vadakkepat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应动作分块（AAC）策略，利用动作熵作为线索，根据当前预测自适应确定分块大小，以平衡VLA模型的响应性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中，动作分块是提高机器人操作能力的关键技术。然而，固定分块大小存在固有矛盾：大分块降低模型对新信息的响应性，小分块增加模式跳跃和动作不连续的风险。当前VLA模型普遍采用经验固定的分块长度，限制了其在多样化操作任务中的优越性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出自适应动作分块（AAC）策略，核心思想是利用动作熵作为自适应确定分块大小的线索。该方法基于当前预测动态调整分块大小，以平衡模型的响应性和动作一致性。

Result: 在广泛的模拟和真实世界机器人操作任务上的实验表明，该方法相比最先进的替代方案显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 自适应动作分块策略有效解决了固定分块大小的局限性，通过动态调整分块大小平衡了VLA模型的响应性和一致性，提升了机器人操作任务的性能。

Abstract: In Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, action chunking (i.e., executing a sequence of actions without intermediate replanning) is a key technique to improve robotic manipulation abilities. However, a large chunk size reduces the model's responsiveness to new information, while a small one increases the likelihood of mode-jumping, jerky behavior resulting from discontinuities between chunks. Therefore, selecting the optimal chunk size is an urgent demand to balance the model's reactivity and consistency. Unfortunately, a dominant trend in current VLA models is an empirical fixed chunk length at inference-time, hindering their superiority and scalability across diverse manipulation tasks. To address this issue, we propose a novel Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) strategy, which exploits action entropy as the cue to adaptively determine the chunk size based on current predictions. Extensive experiments on a wide range of simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks have demonstrated that our approach substantially improves performance over the state-of-the-art alternatives. The videos and source code are publicly available at https://lance-lot.github.io/adaptive-chunking.github.io/.

</details>


### [215] [Primitive-based Truncated Diffusion for Efficient Trajectory Generation of Differential Drive Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04166)
*Long Xu,Choilam Wong,Yuhang Zhong,Junxiao Lin,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于差动驱动移动机械臂的学习增强运动规划器，通过关键点序列提取和基于基元的截断扩散模型提高规划效率、成功率和最优性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划方法在处理复杂3D环境中的移动机械臂任务时，在效率、成功率和轨迹最优性方面存在不足，需要更有效的学习增强规划方法

Method: 1) 关键点序列提取模块：通过可微分前向运动学将边界状态映射到3D空间；2) 点云和关键点分别编码并通过注意力机制融合；3) 基于基元的截断扩散模型：从偏置分布中采样，提高效率和多样性；4) 去噪路径通过轨迹优化进行细化以确保动态可行性和任务特定最优性

Result: 在杂乱3D仿真环境中，相比原始扩散模型和经典基线方法，该方法获得了更高的成功率、改进的轨迹多样性和具有竞争力的运行时间

Conclusion: 提出的学习增强运动规划器通过有效的任务表示编码和基于基元的截断扩散模型，显著提高了移动机械臂在复杂环境中的规划性能，为机器人运动规划提供了新的解决方案

Abstract: We present a learning-enhanced motion planner for differential drive mobile manipulators to improve efficiency, success rate, and optimality. For task representation encoder, we propose a keypoint sequence extraction module that maps boundary states to 3D space via differentiable forward kinematics. Point clouds and keypoints are encoded separately and fused with attention, enabling effective integration of environment and boundary states information. We also propose a primitive-based truncated diffusion model that samples from a biased distribution. Compared with vanilla diffusion model, this framework improves the efficiency and diversity of the solution. Denoised paths are refined by trajectory optimization to ensure dynamic feasibility and task-specific optimality. In cluttered 3D simulations, our method achieves higher success rate, improved trajectory diversity, and competitive runtime compared to vanilla diffusion and classical baselines. The source code is released at https://github.com/nmoma/nmoma .

</details>


### [216] [Robots Need Some Education: On the complexity of learning in evolutionary robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04196)
*Fuda van Diggelen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了进化机器人与机器人学习两个领域的区别与整合，重点研究了在进化机器人背景下开发合适的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化机器人和机器人学习都致力于自动优化机器人设计，但两者在优化对象和时间尺度上存在差异。整合这两个领域需要精心设计适合进化机器人背景的学习算法，而引入学习对进化过程的影响尚未得到充分理解，这构成了研究的动机。

Method: 论文研究了在进化机器人背景下开发多种学习算法，探讨了将学习整合到进化过程中的复杂性和挑战。

Result: 论文提出了多个专门为进化机器人背景开发的学习算法，深入分析了学习与进化整合的机制和效果。

Conclusion: 进化机器人与机器人学习的整合需要精心设计的学习算法，该研究为理解学习在进化过程中的作用提供了重要见解，并开发了适用于这一背景的算法框架。

Abstract: Evolutionary Robotics and Robot Learning are two fields in robotics that aim to automatically optimize robot designs. The key difference between them lies in what is being optimized and the time scale involved. Evolutionary Robotics is a field that applies evolutionary computation techniques to evolve the morphologies or controllers, or both. Robot Learning, on the other hand, involves any learning technique aimed at optimizing a robot's controller in a given morphology. In terms of time scales, evolution occurs across multiple generations, whereas learning takes place within the `lifespan' of an individual robot. Integrating Robot Learning with Evolutionary Robotics requires the careful design of suitable learning algorithms in the context of evolutionary robotics. The effects of introducing learning into the evolutionary process are not well-understood and can thus be tricky. This thesis investigates these intricacies and presents several learning algorithms developed for an Evolutionary Robotics context.

</details>


### [217] [RK-MPC: Residual Koopman Model Predictive Control for Quadruped Locomotion in Offroad Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04221)
*Sriram S. K. S. Narayanan,Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RK-MPC是一种基于Koopman算子的数据驱动模型预测控制框架，通过残差学习提升四足机器人运动预测精度，同时保持实时计算能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在非结构化环境中运动控制时，传统模板模型因接触变化和地形扰动导致的模型失配问题，同时保持优化控制的结构优势和约束处理能力

Method: 在名义模板模型基础上，从数据中学习紧凑的线性残差预测器，嵌入凸二次规划MPC框架中，实现500Hz的板载实时控制

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和Unitree Go1硬件实验中，RK-MPC能够可靠地在接触扰动、多种步态和具有挑战性的非结构化地形（草地、碎石、雪地、冰面）上实现盲运动

Conclusion: RK-MPC为四足机器人在非结构化环境中的数据驱动预测控制提供了一条实用且经过硬件验证的途径，残差校正提高了多步预测和闭环性能，同时降低了对可观测基选择的敏感性

Abstract: This paper presents Residual Koopman MPC (RK-MPC), a Koopman-based, data-driven model predictive control framework for quadruped locomotion that improves prediction fidelity while preserving real-time tractability. RK-MPC augments a nominal template model with a compact linear residual predictor learned from data in lifted coordinates, enabling systematic correction of model mismatch induced by contact variability and terrain disturbances with provable bounds on multi-step prediction error. The learned residual model is embedded within a convex quadratic-program MPC formulation, yielding a receding-horizon controller that runs onboard at 500 Hz and retains the structure and constraint-handling advantages of optimization-based control. We evaluate RK-MPC in both Gazebo simulation and Unitree Go1 hardware experiments, demonstrating reliable blind locomotion across contact disturbances, multiple gait schedules, and challenging off-road terrains including grass, gravel, snow, and ice. We further compare against Koopman/EDMD baselines using alternative observable dictionaries, including monomial and $SE(3)$-structured bases, and show that the residual correction improves multi-step prediction and closed-loop performance while reducing sensitivity to the choice of observables. Overall, RK-MPC provides a practical, hardware-validated pathway for data-driven predictive control of quadrupeds in unstructured environments. See https://sriram-2502.github.io/rk-mpc for implementation videos.

</details>


### [218] [frax: Fast Robot Kinematics and Dynamics in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04310)
*Daniel Morton,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: frax是一个基于JAX的机器人运动学和动力学库，提供跨CPU、GPU和TPU的高性能纯Python接口，支持实时控制和并行化，在CPU上达到微秒级计算时间，在GPU上可扩展到数千个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人动力学库通常在低延迟CPU执行或高吞吐量GPU工作负载方面表现优异，但缺乏能够在多种架构上提供统一框架而不牺牲性能或易用性的解决方案。需要一种既高性能又易于使用、兼容CPU和加速器的刚体动力学库。

Method: 开发了frax，这是一个基于JAX的机器人运动学和动力学库，采用完全向量化的机器人动力学方法，提供纯Python接口，支持自动微分，可在CPU、GPU和TPU上运行。

Result: 在CPU上，frax实现微秒级计算时间，适合千赫兹控制频率，性能优于常见Python库并接近优化的C++实现。在GPU上，相同代码可扩展到数千个实例，达到每秒超过1亿次动力学评估。在Franka Panda机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了性能。

Conclusion: frax提供了一个高性能、易于使用的统一框架，支持多种计算架构，满足机器人控制、规划和学习中实时控制和并行化的需求，已作为开源库发布。

Abstract: In robot control, planning, and learning, there is a need for rigid-body dynamics libraries that are highly performant, easy to use, and compatible with CPUs and accelerators. While existing libraries often excel at either low-latency CPU execution or high-throughput GPU workloads, few provide a unified framework that targets multiple architectures without compromising performance or ease-of-use. To address this, we introduce frax, a JAX-based library for robot kinematics and dynamics, providing a high-performance, pure-Python interface across CPU, GPU, and TPU. Via a fully-vectorized approach to robot dynamics, frax enables efficient real-time control and parallelization, while supporting automatic differentiation for optimization-based methods. On CPU, frax achieves low-microsecond computation times suitable for kilohertz control rates, outperforming common libraries in Python and approaching optimized C++ implementations. On GPU, the same code scales to thousands of instances, reaching upwards of 100 million dynamics evaluations per second. We validate performance on a Franka Panda manipulator and a Unitree G1 humanoid, and release frax as an open-source library.

</details>


### [219] [Towards Considerate Human-Robot Coexistence: A Dual-Space Framework of Robot Design and Human Perception in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04374)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Ruixiang Han,Niti Parikh,Wendy Ju,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过深度访谈探讨人机共存中人类感知的动态演化，提出人类感知空间的概念框架，并构建了人类感知空间与机器人设计空间相互演化的循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人技术快速发展，人机共存已超越物理空间共享，呈现出组织嵌入性、时间演化性、社会情境性和开放不确定性等特征。然而，现有研究多关注静态的态度和接受度快照，缺乏对人类感知如何形成和演化以及人类如何主动塑造共存动态过程的深入理解。

Method: 通过对14周医疗机器人协同设计研究中9名参与者进行深度后续访谈，识别人类感知空间及其四个解释维度（分解程度、时间取向、推理范围、证据来源），并构建人类感知空间与机器人设计空间相互演化的概念框架。

Result: 提出了人类感知空间的概念框架，包含四个解释维度；构建了人类感知空间与机器人设计空间相互演化的循环模型，其中人类需求、设计决策、情境解释和社会中介随时间不断重塑彼此；提出了"体贴的人机共存"概念，强调人类不仅是设计贡献者，更是解释者和中介者，主动塑造机器人如何在不同部署阶段被理解和整合。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了人机共存的概念框架，强调人类感知的动态性和人类在塑造人机共存中的主动角色，为设计更体贴、适应性更强的人机交互系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of robotics, spanning expanded capabilities, more intuitive interaction, and more integration into real-world workflows, is reshaping what it means for humans and robots to coexist. Beyond sharing physical space, this coexistence is increasingly characterized by organizational embeddedness, temporal evolution, social situatedness, and open-ended uncertainty. However, prior work has largely focused on static snapshots of attitudes and acceptance, offering limited insight into how perceptions form and evolve, and what active role humans play in shaping coexistence as a dynamic process. We address these gaps through in-depth follow-up interviews with nine participants from a 14-week co-design study on healthcare robots. We identify the human perception space, including four interpretive dimensions (i.e., degree of decomposition, temporal orientation, scope of reasoning, and source of evidence). We enrich the conceptual framework of human-robot coexistence by conceptualizing the mutual relationship between the human perception space and the robot design space as a co-evolving loop, in which human needs, design decisions, situated interpretations, and social mediation continuously reshape one another over time. Building on this, we propose considerate human-robot coexistence, arguing that humans act not only as design contributors but also as interpreters and mediators who actively shape how robots are understood and integrated across deployment stages.

</details>


### [220] [ReinVBC: A Model-based Reinforcement Learning Approach to Vehicle Braking Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04401)
*Haoxin Lin,Junjie Zhou,Daheng Xu,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReinVBC方法，将离线模型强化学习应用于车辆制动控制，通过工程化设计获得可靠的车辆动力学模型和制动策略，有望替代生产级防抱死制动系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆制动系统作为确保安全性和可操控性的关键模块，在生产过程中依赖大量人工校准。在保持车辆制动控制器性能的同时减少劳动力和时间消耗，对汽车行业具有重要价值

Method: 采用离线模型强化学习方法，在数据驱动的动力学模型中进行策略探索。引入工程化设计到模型学习和利用范式中，以获得可靠的车辆动力学模型和有效的制动策略

Result: 多个结果表明该方法在实际车辆制动中具有良好能力，并显示出替代生产级防抱死制动系统的潜力

Conclusion: ReinVBC方法通过离线模型强化学习成功解决了车辆制动控制问题，其工程化设计确保了模型的可靠性和策略的有效性，为汽车制动系统的自动化校准提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: Braking system, the key module to ensure the safety and steer-ability of current vehicles, relies on extensive manual calibration during production. Reducing labor and time consumption while maintaining the Vehicle Braking Controller (VBC) performance greatly benefits the vehicle industry. Model-based methods in offline reinforcement learning, which facilitate policy exploration within a data-driven dynamics model, offer a promising solution for addressing real-world control tasks. This work proposes ReinVBC, which applies an offline model-based reinforcement learning approach to deal with the vehicle braking control problem. We introduce useful engineering designs into the paradigm of model learning and utilization to obtain a reliable vehicle dynamics model and a capable braking policy. Several results demonstrate the capability of our method in real-world vehicle braking and its potential to replace the production-grade anti-lock braking system.

</details>


### [221] [Veo-Act: How Far Can Frontier Video Models Advance Generalizable Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04502)
*Zhongru Zhang,Chenghan Yang,Qingzhou Lu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianke Zhang,Yucheng Hu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索先进视频生成模型Veo-3在机器人操作中的零样本应用，发现其能生成合理任务级轨迹但控制精度不足，进而提出分层框架Veo-Act结合VLA策略提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频生成模型的快速发展，它们开始展现出对物理动力学的深刻理解。本研究旨在探索先进视频生成模型（如Veo-3）在通用机器人操作任务中的潜力，特别是通过零样本方式将视觉预测转化为可执行的机器人动作。

Method: 首先采用零样本方法：Veo-3从当前机器人观测预测未来图像序列，同时逆动力学模型IDM从随机游戏数据中学习恢复相应机器人动作。随后提出分层框架Veo-Act：使用Veo-3作为高层运动规划器，结合视觉-语言-动作策略作为低层执行器。

Result: Veo-3+IDM方法能在仿真和真实世界的高维灵巧手任务中生成近似正确的任务级轨迹，但由于低层控制精度不足，无法可靠解决大多数任务。Veo-Act框架显著提升了最先进视觉-语言-动作策略的指令跟随性能。

Conclusion: 前沿视频模型具有强大的泛化能力，能够支持机器人学习。随着视频生成模型的持续改进，它们有望成为通用机器人学习的有价值组件。分层方法结合高层规划与低层执行能有效弥补纯视觉预测在控制精度上的不足。

Abstract: Video generation models have advanced rapidly and are beginning to show a strong understanding of physical dynamics. In this paper, we investigate how far an advanced video generation model such as Veo-3 can support generalizable robotic manipulation. We first study a zero-shot approach in which Veo-3 predicts future image sequences from current robot observations, while an inverse dynamics model IDM recovers the corresponding robot actions. The IDM is trained solely on random-play data, requiring neither human supervision nor expert demonstrations. The key intuition is that, if a video model can generate physically plausible future motions in image space, an IDM can translate those visual trajectories into executable robot actions. We evaluate this "Veo-3+IDM" approach in both simulation and the real world using a high-dimensional dexterous hand. We find that, owing to the strong generalization capability of frontier video models, Veo-3+IDM can consistently generate approximately correct task-level trajectories. However, its low-level control accuracy remains insufficient to solve most tasks reliably. Motivated by this observation, we develop a hierarchical framework, Veo-Act, which uses Veo-3 as a high-level motion planner and a VLA policy as the low-level executor, significantly improving the instruction-following performance of a state-of-the-art vision-language-action policy. Overall, our results suggest that, as video generation models continue to improve, video models can be a valuable component for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [222] [G-EDF-Loc: 3D Continuous Gaussian Distance Field for Robust Gradient-Based 6DoF Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04525)
*José E. Maese,Lucía Coto-Elena,Luis Merino,Fernando Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于G-EDF（连续内存高效3D距离场）的6-DoF直接扫描到地图配准定位框架，实现实时高精度定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发一种鲁棒的6自由度定位系统，能够在里程计严重退化或完全缺乏IMU先验的情况下保持稳定性能，同时实现实时计算效率

Method: 采用基于CPU的直接扫描到地图配准流水线，提出G-EDF连续距离场表示，使用块稀疏高斯混合模型配合自适应空间划分，确保C^1连续性并消除边界伪影，利用保持Eikonal一致性的解析梯度

Result: 在大规模数据集上实验表明，G-EDF-Loc与最先进方法性能相当，在严重里程计退化或完全无IMU先验情况下表现出卓越的鲁棒性

Conclusion: G-EDF提供了一种连续且内存高效的3D距离场表示，基于此的定位框架实现了高保真空间重建和实时定位，在挑战性场景中具有优异鲁棒性

Abstract: This paper presents a robust 6-DoF localization framework based on a direct, CPU-based scan-to-map registration pipeline. The system leverages G-EDF, a novel continuous and memory-efficient 3D distance field representation. The approach models the Euclidean Distance Field (EDF) using a Block-Sparse Gaussian Mixture Model with adaptive spatial partitioning, ensuring $C^1$ continuity across block transitions and mitigating boundary artifacts. By leveraging the analytical gradients of this continuous map, which maintain Eikonal consistency, the proposed method achieves high-fidelity spatial reconstruction and real-time localization. Experimental results on large-scale datasets demonstrate that G-EDF-Loc performs competitively against state-of-the-art methods, exhibiting exceptional resilience even under severe odometry degradation or in the complete absence of IMU priors.

</details>


### [223] [Visual Prompt Based Reasoning for Offroad Mapping using Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04564)
*Abdelmoamen Nasser,Yousef Baba'a,Murad Mebrahtu,Nadya Abdel Madjid,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本越野自主导航方法，利用SAM2进行环境分割和视觉语言模型(VLM)推理可行驶区域，无需单独训练地形分类、高度估计等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统越野自主导航需要分别训练地形分类、高度估计和滑移/坡度量化等多个模型，这需要针对每个组件单独训练、使用特定任务数据集并进行微调，过程复杂且资源密集。

Method: 使用SAM2进行环境分割生成带数字标签的掩码图像，将原始图像和标注后的分割图像输入视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过提示让VLM识别哪些数字标签对应的区域是可行驶的，结合规划和控制系统形成统一框架。

Result: 该方法在高分辨率分割数据集上超越了最先进的可训练模型，并在Isaac Sim越野环境中实现了完整的堆栈导航，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该零样本方法利用VLM的固有推理能力，消除了对显式地形特定模型的需求，为越野自主导航提供了一种更简单、更统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to off-road autonomy rely on separate models for terrain classification, height estimation, and quantifying slip or slope conditions. Utilizing several models requires training each component separately, having task specific datasets, and fine-tuning. In this work, we present a zero-shot approach leveraging SAM2 for environment segmentation and a vision-language model (VLM) to reason about drivable areas. Our approach involves passing to the VLM both the original image and the segmented image annotated with numeric labels for each mask. The VLM is then prompted to identify which regions, represented by these numeric labels, are drivable. Combined with planning and control modules, this unified framework eliminates the need for explicit terrain-specific models and relies instead on the inherent reasoning capabilities of the VLM. Our approach surpasses state-of-the-art trainable models on high resolution segmentation datasets and enables full stack navigation in our Isaac Sim offroad environment.

</details>


### [224] [Biologically Inspired Event-Based Perception and Sample-Efficient Learning for High-Speed Table Tennis Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04618)
*Ziqi Wang,Jingyue Zhao,Xun Xiao,Jichao Yang,Yaohua Wang,Shi Xu,Lei Wang,Huadong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受生物启发的乒乓球机器人方法，结合事件感知与样本高效学习，解决高速动态场景下的感知与决策挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的视觉传感器在高速动态场景中存在运动模糊、高延迟和数据冗余问题，难以满足实时准确感知需求；现有事件感知方法仅限于简化的纯球场景，而现有决策方法需要大量环境交互，计算成本高

Method: 1) 感知端：提出基于事件的球检测方法，利用运动线索和几何一致性，直接在异步事件流上操作，无需帧重建；2) 决策端：提出受人类启发的样本高效训练策略，先在低速场景训练策略，逐步从基础到高级获取技能，然后通过案例依赖的时间自适应奖励和奖励阈值机制适应高速场景

Result: 在相同训练回合数下，方法将回球到目标位置的准确率提高了35.8%，证明了生物启发感知与决策在高速机器人系统中的有效性

Conclusion: 结合事件感知与样本高效学习的生物启发方法能够有效解决高速动态场景中的感知与决策挑战，为高速机器人系统提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: Perception and decision-making in high-speed dynamic scenarios remain challenging for current robots. In contrast, humans and animals can rapidly perceive and make decisions in such environments. Taking table tennis as a typical example, conventional frame-based vision sensors suffer from motion blur, high latency and data redundancy, which can hardly meet real-time, accurate perception requirements. Inspired by the human visual system, event-based perception methods address these limitations through asynchronous sensing, high temporal resolution, and inherently sparse data representations. However, current event-based methods are still restricted to simplified, unrealistic ball-only scenarios. Meanwhile, existing decision-making approaches typically require thousands of interactions with the environment to converge, resulting in significant computational costs. In this work, we present a biologically inspired approach for high-speed table tennis robots, combining event-based perception with sample-efficient learning. On the perception side, we propose an event-based ball detection method that leverages motion cues and geometric consistency, operating directly on asynchronous event streams without frame reconstruction, to achieve robust and efficient detection in real-world rallies. On the decision-making side, we introduce a human-inspired, sample-efficient training strategy that first trains policies in low-speed scenarios, progressively acquiring skills from basic to advanced, and then adapts them to high-speed scenarios, guided by a case-dependent temporally adaptive reward and a reward-threshold mechanism.
